# Skills · J · part 2

730 entries, jam-me-up-docs to jarvis-delegate. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **jam-me-up-docs**: Adversarial stress-test skill with full domain awareness. Stops the user cold and forces hard answers while enforcing glossary consistency against CONTEXT.md, sharpening fuzzy terminology, cross-referencing code reality, and creating ADRs for irreversible decisions. Uses structured multiple-choice questions with 3+ concrete options plus a free-text "Explain..." escape hatch. Explores codebase and docs first. Activate when the user says "jam me up with docs", "grill me with docs", "stress test against context", or "grill with context". Never activate for bug fixes or gentle clarification. ([m5trevino/skillz-bank](https://github.com/m5trevino/skillz-bank/tree/HEAD/jam-me-up-docs))
- **jam-mode**: Game jam workflow — locks the project into shippable scope for a fixed time budget (48h, 72h, week). Forces CC0 asset sources, kills feature creep at hour 24, auto-generates devlog updates, and enforces a 'ship at hour T-6' checkpoint. Trigger when the user joins or starts a game jam, has a hard time budget, or says 'I need to finish in N days'. ([ivala2081/vibe-game](https://github.com/ivala2081/vibe-game/tree/HEAD/skills/jam-mode) · ★4)
- **jam-model**: Generate a 3D mesh of one asset from its accepted turnaround through the project's mesh route, with a priced and recorded job, a download of the originals, and an inspection against every view. Use when the user asks to generate, make or build the 3D model or mesh of an asset, when a mesh generation job must be resumed or settled, or when a run's next step names this skill. ([RafaelVidaurre/furanku-skills](https://github.com/RafaelVidaurre/furanku-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jam-model) · ★2)
- **jam-prep-checklist**: setup preparation readiness checklist pre-jam verification launch ready ([karchtho/my-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/karchtho/my-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/bundles/game-jam-toolkit-bundle/skills/jam-prep-checklist))
- **jam-proof**: Record work you can see on screen with `jam record` and publish it as a Jam link (a shareable jam.dev recording with the console and network attached). Use when the user asks for a recorded Jam, a shareable demo, or video proof of work in a browser or desktop app. A request to verify a change or give sign-off alone does not call for recording or uploading. ([jamdotdev/skills](https://github.com/jamdotdev/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jam-proof) · ★12)
- **jam-proof-table**: At the end of a batch of agent work (several fixes, a test run, a set of tickets), return one table that ties each claim to the Jam (a jam.dev screen recording with the console and network attached) that shows it, with its status and link, and file those Jams so they can be found later. Use when the user asks "give me the jams", "which jam shows this", "what was the jam I gave you", "show proof for each", or when a run that recorded Jams finishes and the user wants the proof listed. ([jamdotdev/skills](https://github.com/jamdotdev/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/jam-proof-table) · ★12)

Use when the user asks to make a 3D asset, prop, character or model, a sound effect or a voiced line; when a brief names a recipe or a run id; when checking or resuming work in progress; when existing or bought files should enter the workflow part-way; when a project needs its own steps, gates or checks; or when a workflow that was just improvised should become repeatable. Use when asked to reduce the polycount, decimate, remesh or retopologize a generated, scanned, bought or hand-made mesh, to make a quad mesh for something that deforms, to check whether an existing mesh fits its budget, or when a run's next step is topology. - **jam-review**: Review a Jam recording (a jam.dev link: a recorded browser tab with its console, network requests, user actions and video) and turn it into an evidence-based finding: what happened, where it went wrong, how to reproduce it, and what to look at next. Use whenever someone shares a `jam.dev/c/...` link or says "look at this jam", "check this recording", "why did this break, here's the jam" — even without saying "review". Use when the user asks to rig, skin or make animatable a character mesh (generated, bought or hand-made), to re-rig after its mesh changed, to check an existing rig's deformation, or when a recipe run reaches its rig step. - **japanese-proofreader**: Markdownファイル(.md)の日本語テキストを校正し、誤字脱字・文法の誤り・表記揺れを検出して保守的に修正するスキル。 修正結果はファイルに直接反映し、変更箇所をgit diff風の差分で標準出力する。 このスキルは、ユーザーが日本語の.mdファイルの誤りを修正・チェック・確認したいときに必ず使用すること。 具体的には以下のようなケースでトリガーする： - 「校正して」「日本語チェック」「誤字脱字を直して」「表記揺れを確認して」「文章を確認して」「日本語を修正して」 - 「proofread」「typo」「日本語がおかしい」「日本語を見て」「日本語を見直して」 - .mdファイルの日本語テキストに誤りがないか確認・修正・チェックしてほしいという要求全般 - 助詞の誤り、漢字の誤変換、送り仮名の間違い、カタカナ語の長音符号の不統一など、日本語の正確さに関する依頼 - 日本語を含むMarkdownファイルを編集した際のhooksとしての自動実行 文体変更（である調→ですます調）、内容のリライト、英語のスペルチェック、Markdownフォーマット修正、 新規ドキュメント作成、翻訳には使用しない。これらは校正ではなく別のタスクである。 ([dskst/dotclaude](https://github.com/dskst/dotclaude/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/japanese-proofreader))

- **japanese-review**: 日本語の文書を tanteki と yomiyasu の組で執筆・レビューするスキル。tanteki で構成と内容を整えて textlint を通し、yomiyasu で AI が書いたような不自然な日本語を直し、最後に tanteki の textlint だけを再実行して検査が崩れていないことを確かめる。create-pr、renovate-pr-summary、create-adr、create-prd、create-design-doc、create-test-cases、code-to-adr、code-to-prd、create-skill から呼ばれる。ユーザーが「tanteki と yomiyasu で見て」「/japanese-review」と組での確認を明示した場合にも使う。tanteki だけ、yomiyasu だけを指定された場合と、日本語以外の文書には使わない。 ([mikinovation/mikinovation](https://github.com/mikinovation/mikinovation/tree/HEAD/packages/claude-skills/skills/japanese-review) · ★1)
- **japanese-review**: 日本語で書いた文書(PRのタイトル・Description、リリースノート、Notionのメモ、メール、議事録など)を書き終えたあとに、Gemini系の外部レビュアーとセルフレビューで校正するスキル。Antigravity(gemini MCPのask)を優先し、使えなければgemini-api CLI、それも使えなければ自分でレビューする。「日本語をレビューして」「文章をGeminiに見てもらって」「校正して」や、日本語の文書を書き終えた場面で使う。 ([mecaota/skills](https://github.com/mecaota/skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/japanese-review/skills/japanese-review))
- **japanese-rewrite**: AI が書いた日本語の下書きを、読み手が「人が書いた」と感じる自然な日本語に全面的に書き直す。テンプレ感、説明書感、記号過多、過剰な丁寧さ、逃げ文句、抽象語の空回り、「〜ではなく〜」の対比、「〜することで〜が可能に」の因果連結、「鍵」「ポイント」での予告、末尾の「まとめ」といった AI っぽさを消し、逆に「ですね」「なんです」のような口語で人らしさを装うことも避ける。トリガー例は「自然な日本語に直して」「AI っぽさを消して」「人が書いたように書き直して」「リライトして」「この文章を整えて」「文章を推敲して」など。日本語の文章の書き直し・推敲・リライトを頼まれたら、明示的に「AI っぽさ」という言葉がなくても必ずこのスキルを使う。英語の文章、コード、議事録の逐語起こしには使わない。 ([s-akm/claude-kit](https://github.com/s-akm/claude-kit/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/japanese-rewrite))
- **japanese-romanization**: ALA-LC Japanese romanization rules for macrons, hyphens, and word division. Use when writing Japanese words in romaji. ([nikolaj-lat/World-Puppeteer](https://github.com/nikolaj-lat/World-Puppeteer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/japanese-romanization) · ★17)
- **japanese-sentence-order**: 日本語の一文の中の語順、読点の位置、入れ子構造を確かめ、読みにくい文を書き換え案つきで指摘する。ブログ記事、設計書、Issue のコメント、README、コミットメッセージなど、日本語の文章全般に使う。「この文が読みにくい」「修飾語の順番を確認して」「読点の位置を直したい」「一文が長くて分かりにくい」と言われたとき、および係り受けが曖昧な文を見つけたときに使う。文体、構成、使わない言い回しの確認は article-review が担う。 ([Suntory-N-Water/suntory-n-water-marketplace](https://github.com/Suntory-N-Water/suntory-n-water-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/blog-writing/skills/japanese-sentence-order))
- **japanese-slide-writing**: 日本語のスライドを作成・編集・推敲するとき、自然な表現、用語統一、比較軸、図と主張の整合を補う。既存のPresentations等と併用する文章品質スキル。一般の会話・メール・論文本文や、文章変更を伴わないスライド操作には適用しない。 ([z0lw/codex-skills](https://github.com/z0lw/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/japanese-slide-writing))
- **japanese-smart-home-command-normalizer**: Normalize short Japanese smart-home voice transcripts after STT into safe structured intents and slots. - **japanese-speaking**: Writes Japanese meant to be heard, not read, such as a podcast or radio dialogue between hosts, a news item one host reads and both then discuss, an interview, a narration, or a talk script, so that it sounds like people talking instead of reading lines. It checks the draft with a script for patterns that sound read aloud from paper, has a fresh reader listen for lines that still sound read, and fixes both. Use when the user wants a script, dialogue, narration, or anything a person or a text-to-speech engine will say in Japanese. ([cardene777/agent-shuriken](https://github.com/cardene777/agent-shuriken/tree/HEAD/plugins/japanese-speaking/skills/japanese-speaking) · ★1)
- **japanese-specialist**: 日本語文書を新規作成・添削・検証し、文体、誤字脱字、冗長表現、表記ゆらぎ、構成を整える。README、仕様書、手順書、記事などを扱う。コードコメントだけの修正や他言語文書は対象外。 ([goldeneggg/dotfiles](https://github.com/goldeneggg/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/ai-linux/.claude/skills/japanese-specialist) · ★1)
- **japanese-sts-ir-eval**: Evaluates Japanese sentence embeddings on domain-specific semantic textual similarity (STS) and information retrieval (IR) tasks. - **japanese-teacher**: Use this skill for Japanese language learning, JLPT preparation at any level (N5 through N1), and structured language acquisition. - **japanese-teacher**: Act as a personal Japanese language teacher and tutor.
